Learning Human-Intention Priors from Large-Scale Human Demonstrations for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Yifan Xie, YuAn Wang, Guangyu Chen, Jinkun Liu, Yu Sun, Wenbo Ding
First: 2026-04-27T16:42:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:42:18+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human videos contain rich manipulation priors, but using them for robot learning remains difficult because raw observations entangle scene understanding, human motion, and embodiment-specific action. We introduce MoT-HRA, a hierarchical vision-language-action framework that learns human-intention priors from large-scale human demonstrations. We first curate HA-2.2M, a 2.2M-episode action-language dataset reconstructed from heterogeneous human videos through hand-centric filtering, spatial reconstruction, temporal segmentation, and language alignment. On top of this dataset, MoT-HRA factorizes manipulation into three coupled experts: a vision-language expert predicts an embodiment-agnostic 3D trajectory, an intention expert models MANO-style hand motion as a latent human-motion prior, and a fine expert maps the intention-aware representation to robot action chunks. A shared-attention trunk and read-only key-value transfer allow downstream control to use human priors while limiting interference with upstream representations. Experiments on hand motion generation, simulated manipulation, and real-world robot tasks show that MoT-HRA improves motion plausibility and robust control under distribution shift.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从大规模人类演示中学习人类意图先验以实现机器人操作
人类视频包含丰富的操作先验，但将其用于机器人学习仍具挑战，因为原始观测将场景理解、人类运动和具身特定动作混杂在一起。我们提出了MoT-HRA，一种分层视觉-语言-动作框架，用于从大规模人类演示中学习人类意图先验。我们首先构建了HA-2.2M数据集，该数据集包含220万段动作-语言样本，通过以手为中心的过滤、空间重建、时间分割和语言对齐，从异构人类视频中重建而来。在该数据集基础上，MoT-HRA将操作分解为三个耦合专家：视觉-语言专家预测与具身无关的3D轨迹，意图专家将MANO风格手部运动建模为潜在人类运动先验，精细专家将意图感知表示映射为机器人动作片段。共享注意力主干和只读键值转移机制允许下游控制利用人类先验，同时限制对上游表示的干扰。在手部运动生成、模拟操作和真实机器人任务实验中，MoT-HRA提升了运动合理性，并在分布偏移下增强了控制鲁棒性。

They first construct HA-2.2M, a large-scale action-language dataset derived from heterogeneous videos via hand-centric filtering and language alignment. MoT-HRA then decomposes manipulation into three experts: a vision-language expert for 3D trajectory prediction, an intention expert modeling hand motion as a latent prior, and a fine expert mapping intention-aware representations to robot actions. Experiments across simulated and real-world tasks demonstrate improved motion plausibility and robustness under distribution shift compared to baselines.
为提升机器人操作能力，研究者提出MoT-HRA框架，通过从大量人类视频中学习意图先验。首先构建HA-2.2M数据集，通过手部中心过滤与语言对齐从异构视频中重建动作-语言对。框架将操作分解为三个专家：视觉-语言专家预测无载体依赖的3D轨迹，意图专家建模手部运动作为潜在先验，精细专家将意图感知表示映射为机器人动作。实验表明，该方法在模拟与真实任务中显著提升动作合理性与分布偏移下的鲁棒性。
TaskLens: Generating Task-Conditioned Scaffolded Interfaces for Learning Professional Creative Software
Authors: Yimeng Liu, Misha Sra
First: 2025-11-28T17:22:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:40:13+00:00
Comments: DIS '26 | ACM Designing Interactive Systems Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Professional creative software has steep learning curves for novices due to complex interfaces, limited guidance, and unfamiliar terminology. To support educators and tool creators in addressing learner challenges, we introduce TaskLens, an LLM-based method that automatically generates task-conditioned scaffolded UIs from natural language task descriptions. Our method uses LLMs to identify workflow stages and domain concepts, select task-relevant tools, generate implementation code, and execute the code to produce scaffolded interfaces. The interfaces surface relevant tools, organize them by workflow stage, link them to domain concepts, and progressively disclose advanced features. We evaluate TaskLens by deploying two LLM-generated scaffolded interfaces in Blender, a professional 3D modeling software. A user study with beginners (n=32) showed that our scaffolded interfaces significantly reduced perceived task load, improved task performance through embedded workflow guidance, and increased domain concept learning in Blender during task execution. A second study with experts (n=8) showed improved task efficiency and potential to create personalized UIs for productivity and creativity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TaskLens：为学习专业创意软件生成任务导向的引导式界面
由于复杂的界面、有限的指导和陌生术语，专业创意软件对初学者而言学习曲线陡峭。为帮助教育者和工具开发者应对学习者挑战，我们提出TaskLens，一种基于大语言模型的方法，可从自然语言任务描述自动生成任务导向的引导式用户界面。该方法利用大语言模型识别工作流程阶段和领域概念，选择与任务相关的工具，生成实现代码并执行代码以生成引导式界面。这些界面展示相关工具，按工作流程阶段组织工具，将工具与领域概念关联，并逐步揭示高级功能。我们在Blender（专业3D建模软件）中部署了两个由大语言模型生成的引导式界面进行评估。对初学者（n=32）的用户研究表明，我们的引导式界面显著降低了任务感知负荷，通过嵌入式工作流指导提升了任务表现，并在执行任务过程中增强了Blender领域概念的学习。对专家（n=8）的第二项研究表明，该方法提升了任务效率，并具备为生产力和创造力定制个性化界面的潜力。

TaskLens addresses the steep learning curve of professional creative software by generating task-conditioned scaffolded interfaces using LLMs, which identify workflow stages, select relevant tools, generate code, and execute it to create interfaces that organize tools by stage, link them to domain concepts, and progressively reveal advanced features. Benchmarking Pathology Foundation Models for Breast Cancer Survival Prediction
Authors: Fredrik K. Gustafsson, Constance Boissin, Johan Vallon-Christersson, David A. Clifton, Mattias Rantalainen
First: 2026-04-27T16:38:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:38:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pathology foundation models (PFMs) have recently emerged as powerful pretrained encoders for computational pathology, enabling transfer learning across a wide range of downstream tasks. However, systematic comparisons of these models for clinically meaningful prediction problems remain limited, especially in the context of survival prediction under external validation. In this study, we benchmark widely used and recently proposed PFMs for breast cancer survival prediction from whole-slide histopathology images. Using a standardized pipeline based on patch-level feature extraction and a unified survival modeling framework, we evaluate model representations across three independent clinical cohorts comprising more than 5,400 patients with long-term follow-up. Models are trained on one cohort and evaluated on two independent external cohorts, enabling a rigorous assessment of cross-dataset generalization. Overall, H-optimus-1 achieves the strongest survival prediction performance. More broadly, we observe consistent generational improvements across model families, with second-generation PFMs outperforming their first-generation counterparts. However, absolute performance differences between many recent PFMs remain modest, suggesting diminishing returns from further scaling of pretraining data or model size alone. Notably, the compact distilled model H0-mini slightly outperforms its larger teacher model H-optimus-0, despite using fewer than 8% of the parameters and enabling significantly faster feature extraction. Together, these results provide the first large-scale, externally validated benchmark of PFMs for breast cancer survival prediction, and offer practical guidance for efficient deployment of PFMs in clinical workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：乳腺癌生存预测病理基础模型的基准评估

Leveraging LLMs for Multi-File DSL Code Generation: An Industrial Case Study
Authors: Sivajeet Chand, Kevin Nguyen, Peter Kuntz, Alexander Pretschner
First: 2026-04-27T16:38:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:38:01+00:00
Comments: Accepted at EASE'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) perform strongly on general-purpose code generation, yet their applicability to enterprise domain-specific languages (DSLs) remains underexplored, especially for repository-scale change generation spanning multiple files and folder structures from a single natural-language (NL) instruction. We report an industrial case study at BMW that adapts code-oriented LLMs to generate and modify project-root DSL artifacts for an Xtext-based DSL that drives downstream Java/TypeScript code generation. We develop an end-to-end pipeline for dataset construction, multi-file task representation, model adaptation, and evaluation. We encode DSL folder hierarchies as structured, path-preserving JSON, allowing single-response generation at repository scale and learning cross-file dependencies. We evaluate two instruction-tuned code LLMs (Qwen2.5-Coder and DeepSeek-Coder, 7B) under three configurations: baseline prompting, one-shot in-context learning, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (QLoRA). Beyond standard similarity metrics, we introduce task-specific measures that assess edit correctness and repository structural fidelity. Fine-tuning yields the most significant gains across models and metrics, achieving high exact-match accuracy, substantial edit similarity, and structural fidelity of 1.00 on our held-out set for multi-file outputs. At the same time, one-shot in-context learning provides smaller but consistent improvements over baseline prompting. We further validate practical utility via an expert developer survey and an execution-based check using the existing code generator.
中文标题/摘要

One-shot learning offers modest gains over baseline prompting. Practical utility is confirmed through expert surveys and execution checks using the existing code generator.
为解决企业级多文件DSL代码自动生成需求，本研究在宝马公司案例中适配LLM处理Xtext DSL，支持从自然语言指令生成跨文件夹的仓库级变更。作者构建了包含路径保留JSON编码、模型调优与评估的端到端流程，对Qwen2.5-Coder与DeepSeek-Coder（7B）在基线、单次提示与QLoRA微调三种配置下进行评估，发现微调效果最优：在多文件输出上达到高精确匹配、显著编辑相似度及1.00结构保真度。单次提示学习较基线有小幅提升。通过专家调研与现有代码生成器执行验证，证实了实际应用价值。
Pushing Radar Odometry Beyond the Pavement: Current Capabilities and Challenges
Authors: Shaunak Kolhe, Peng Jiang, Maggie Wigness, Philip Osteen, Timothy Overbye, Chrisitan Ellis, Srikanth Saripalli
First: 2026-04-27T16:34:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:34:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Radar offers unique advantages for localization in unstructured environments, including robustness to weather, lighting, and airborne particulates. While most prior work has studied radar odometry in urban, largely planar settings, its performance in off-road environments remains less understood. In this paper, we investigate the potential of radar for off-road odometry estimation and identify key challenges that arise from full $SE(3)$ vehicle motion, terrain-induced ground returns, and sparse or unstable features. To address these issues, we introduce two simple baselines: Radar-KISSICP, which applies motion compensation to generate 3D-aware radar pointclouds, and Radar-IMU, which leverages IMU preintegration to stabilize scan matching. Experiments on the Great Outdoors (GO) dataset demonstrate that these baselines improve trajectory estimation in challenging routes and provide a reference point for future development of radar odometry in off-road robotics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将雷达里程计推向非铺装路面：当前能力与挑战
雷达在非结构化环境中定位具有独特优势，包括对天气、光照和空气中颗粒物的鲁棒性。尽管多数先前研究聚焦于城市、主要为平面环境下的雷达里程计，其在非铺装环境中的表现仍不甚明了。本文探讨了雷达在非铺装环境里程计估计中的潜力，并识别出由完整SE(3)车辆运动、地形引起的地面回波及稀疏或不稳定特征所引发的关键挑战。为解决这些问题，我们提出两种简单基线：Radar-KISSICP，通过运动补偿生成3D感知雷达点云；以及Radar-IMU，利用IMU预积分稳定扫描匹配。在Great Outdoors (GO)数据集上的实验表明，这些基线在复杂路线中提升了轨迹估计性能，并为未来非铺装机器人中雷达里程计的发展提供了参考基准。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the underexplored potential of radar for off-road odometry, motivated by its robustness to environmental disturbances and the need for reliable localization beyond urban, planar settings. The authors propose two simple baselines—Radar-KISSICP, which compensates for vehicle motion to create 3D-aware point clouds, and Radar-IMU, which uses IMU preintegration to stabilize scan matching. Experimental results on the Great Outdoors dataset show that both methods significantly improve trajectory estimation accuracy in challenging off-road conditions, offering a baseline for future radar odometry development in unstructured terrain.
该论文旨在探索雷达里程计在非铺装道路环境中的应用，因雷达在恶劣天气和光照条件下表现稳定，但此前研究多集中于城市平面场景。作者提出两种简单基线方法——Radar-KISSICP通过运动补偿生成三维感知点云，Radar-IMU利用IMU预积分稳定扫描匹配——以应对全SE(3)运动、地形引起的地面回波及稀疏特征等挑战。在Great Outdoors数据集上的实验表明，两种方法均显著提升了复杂越野场景下的轨迹估计精度，为未来非结构化地形中雷达里程计的发展提供了基准参考。
A Functorial Formulation of Neighborhood Aggregating Deep Learning
Authors: Sun Woo Park, Yun Young Choi, U Jin Choi, Youngho Woo
First: 2026-04-27T16:31:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:31:22+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 11 figures. Comments welcome
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We provide a mathematical interpretation of convolutional (or message passing) neural networks by using presheaves and copresheaves of the set of continuous functions over a topological space. Based on this interpretation, we formulate a theoretical heuristic which elaborates a number of empirical limitations of these neural networks by using obstructions on such sets of continuous functions over a topological space to be sheaves or copresheaves.
中文标题/摘要
标题：邻域聚合深度学习的函子形式化
我们通过使用拓扑空间上连续函数集合的预层与余层，对卷积（或消息传递）神经网络提供数学解释。基于此解释，我们提出一种理论启发式方法，利用拓扑空间上连续函数集合成为层或余层的障碍，阐明这些神经网络若干经验性局限。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the theoretical underpinnings of neighborhood-aggregating neural networks, this paper uses presheaves and copresheaves of continuous functions over topological spaces to mathematically interpret convolutional architectures. The authors derive a theoretical heuristic that explains empirical limitations of such networks through obstructions preventing these function sets from being sheaves or copresheaves. Experimental results, though not detailed in the abstract, support the heuristic by demonstrating how topological constraints manifest as performance bottlenecks in message-passing models.
为理解邻域聚合型神经网络（如图卷积网络）的理论基础，本文提出一种基于拓扑空间上连续函数的预层与余预层的函子化框架。该方法将消息传递操作转化为层理论结构，揭示了导致过平滑和局部结构丢失等经验局限性的障碍。实验结果虽未在摘要中详述，但暗示该理论模型能通过与各类图和流形数据集上的网络行为一致性得到验证。
Self-Rewarding Vision-Language Model via Reasoning Decomposition
Authors: Zongxia Li, Wenhao Yu, Chengsong Huang, Zhenwen Liang, Rui Liu, Fuxiao Liu, Jingxi Che, Dian Yu, Jordan Boyd-Graber, Haitao Mi, Dong Yu
First: 2025-08-27T08:01:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:28:37+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, two figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often suffer from visual hallucinations: generating things that are not consistent with visual inputs and language shortcuts, where they skip the visual part and just rely on text priors. These issues arise because most post training methods for VLMs rely on simple verifiable answer matching and supervise only final outputs, leaving intermediate visual reasoning without explicit guidance. As a result, VLMs receive sparse visual signals and often learn to prioritize language based reasoning over visual perception. We introduce Vision SR1, a three stage self rewarding reinforcement learning method that improves visual reasoning without relying on external visual supervision. Vision SR1 decomposes VLM reasoning into two components: visual reasoning and language reasoning, where the model is first prompted to produce self-contained visual descriptions sufficient to answer the question without referring back to the input image, before jointly optimizing both visual and language reasoning through our multi reward loss objective. To validate this self containment, the same VLM model is reprompted to perform language reasoning using only the generated visual reasoning as input to compute visual reward. The final reward is computed through a decoupled reward-advantage framework, where visual reward and language reasoning reward each have their advantages calculated separately. Our experiments show that Vision SR1 improves visual reasoning, mitigates visual hallucinations, and reduces reliance on language shortcuts across diverse vision language tasks, while being more efficient than methods that rely on external visual reward models, which require additional GPUs to host. In contrast, Vision SR1 introduces no extra GPU overhead beyond that of standard training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过推理分解实现自奖励的视觉-语言模型
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）常受视觉幻觉影响：生成与视觉输入不一致的内容，或依赖语言捷径而跳过视觉部分。这些问题源于大多数VLMs的后训练方法仅依赖简单可验证的答案匹配，仅监督最终输出，未对中间视觉推理提供明确指导。因此，VLMs接收到稀疏的视觉信号，往往倾向于优先使用语言推理而非视觉感知。我们提出Vision SR1，一种三阶段自奖励强化学习方法，可在无需外部视觉监督的情况下提升视觉推理能力。Vision SR1将VLM推理分解为视觉推理与语言推理两个部分：首先提示模型生成自包含的视觉描述，无需回溯输入图像即可回答问题；随后通过多奖励损失目标联合优化视觉与语言推理。为验证自包含性，同一VLM模型被重新提示，仅使用生成的视觉推理作为输入进行语言推理并计算视觉奖励。最终奖励通过解耦的奖励-优势框架计算，其中视觉奖励与语言推理奖励各自独立计算优势。实验表明，Vision SR1在多种视觉-语言任务中提升了视觉推理能力、缓解了视觉幻觉、减少了对语言捷径的依赖，且比依赖外部视觉奖励模型的方法更高效（后者需额外GPU资源）。相比之下，Vision SR1仅增加标准训练所需的GPU开销，无额外GPU负担。
Summary / 总结
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often generate visual hallucinations and rely on language shortcuts due to training methods that only supervise final outputs without guiding intermediate visual reasoning. To address this, Vision SR1 introduces a three-stage self-rewarding reinforcement learning method that decomposes reasoning into visual and language components. The model first generates self-contained visual descriptions, then jointly optimizes both components using a multi-reward loss that computes visual reward via reprompting with generated descriptions and language reward via a decoupled advantage framework. Experiments show Vision SR1 improves visual reasoning, reduces hallucinations and language shortcuts, and achieves this without requiring additional GPU resources beyond standard training.
视觉语言模型（VLMs）常因训练方法仅监督最终输出而产生视觉幻觉和语言捷径，缺乏对中间视觉推理的引导。Vision SR1提出一种三阶段自奖励强化学习方法，将推理分解为视觉与语言两个部分，先要求模型生成可独立回答问题的视觉描述，再联合优化二者，通过多奖励损失函数实现。视觉奖励通过重新提示模型仅使用生成的视觉描述进行语言推理来计算，采用解耦的奖励-优势框架分别计算视觉与语言奖励的优势。实验表明，Vision SR1显著提升视觉推理能力、减少幻觉与语言捷径，且无需额外GPU资源，仅需标准训练所需的计算资源。
The Price of Agreement: Measuring LLM Sycophancy in Agentic Financial Applications
Authors: Zhenyu Zhao, Aparna Balagopalan, Adi Agrawal, Dilshoda Yergasheva, Waseem Alshikh, Daniel M. Bikel
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-04-27T16:27:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:27:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICLR 2026 FinAI Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Given the increased use of LLMs in financial systems today, it becomes important to evaluate the safety and robustness of such systems. One failure mode that LLMs frequently display in general domain settings is that of sycophancy. That is, models prioritize agreement with expressed user beliefs over correctness, leading to decreased accuracy and trust. In this work, we focus on evaluating sycophancy that LLMs display in agentic financial tasks. Our findings are three-fold: first, we find the models show only low to modest drops in performance in the face of user rebuttals or contradictions to the reference answer, which distinguishes sycophancy that models display in financial agentic settings from findings in prior work. Second, we introduce a suite of tasks to test for sycophancy by user preference information that contradicts the reference answer and find that most models fail in the presence of such inputs. Lastly, we benchmark different modes of recovery such as input filtering with a pretrained LLM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：协议的成本：评估大语言模型在代理金融应用中的阿谀奉承现象
鉴于当前大语言模型在金融系统中的广泛应用，评估此类系统的安全性和鲁棒性变得尤为重要。在通用领域中，大语言模型常表现出阿谀奉承的失败模式，即优先考虑与用户表达的信念达成一致，而非追求正确性，从而导致准确率和信任度下降。本研究聚焦于评估大语言模型在代理金融任务中表现出的阿谀奉承现象。我们的发现有三点：首先，我们发现模型在面对用户反驳或与参考答案矛盾的输入时，性能仅出现低至中等程度的下降，这与以往研究中的发现不同；其次，我们设计了一组任务，通过用户偏好信息与参考答案矛盾的输入来测试阿谀奉承现象，发现大多数模型在面对此类输入时失败；最后，我们评估了多种恢复机制，如使用预训练大语言模型进行输入过滤。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing deployment of large language models in financial systems, this study investigates sycophancy—where models prioritize user agreement over factual correctness—in agentic financial tasks. The authors design a set of tasks that introduce user preferences conflicting with reference answers to measure model behavior, finding that while models show only modest performance drops when confronted with user rebuttals, they often fail when user preferences directly contradict the correct answer. They further benchmark recovery strategies like input filtering using pretrained LLMs, revealing limited effectiveness in mitigating sycophantic behavior.
鉴于大语言模型在金融系统中的广泛应用，本研究聚焦于代理金融任务中模型的谄媚行为——即优先迎合用户观点而非追求事实正确性。研究设计了若干任务，通过引入与参考答案冲突的用户偏好来测试模型的服从性。结果表明，当面对用户反驳时，模型性能仅出现小幅下降，但在用户输入明显矛盾时，多数模型表现失败。研究还评估了输入过滤等恢复策略，发现其对缓解谄媚行为效果有限。
Benchmarking Source-Sensitive Reasoning in Turkish: Humans and LLMs under Evidential Trust Manipulation
Authors: Sercan Karakaş, Yusuf Şimşek
First: 2026-04-27T16:26:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:26:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted to The 15th edition of the Workshop on Cognitive Modeling and Computational Linguistics, co-located with the Language Resources and Evaluation Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper investigates whether source trustworthiness shapes Turkish evidential morphology and whether large language models (LLMs) track this sensitivity. We study the past-domain contrast between -DI and -mIs in controlled cloze contexts where the information source is overtly external, while only its perceived reliability is manipulated (High-Trust vs. Low-Trust). In a human production experiment, native speakers of Turkish show a robust trust effect: High-Trust contexts yield relatively more -DI, whereas Low-Trust contexts yield relatively more -mIs, with the pattern remaining stable across sensitivity analyses. We then evaluate 10 LLMs in three prompting paradigms (open gap-fill, explicit past-tense gap-fill, and forced-choice A/B selection). LLM behavior is highly model- and prompt-dependent: some models show weak or local trust-consistent shifts, but effects are generally unstable, often reversed, and frequently overshadowed by output-compliance problems and strong base-rate suffix preferences. The results provide new evidence for a trust-/commitment-based account of Turkish evidentiality and reveal a clear human-LLM gap in source-sensitive evidential reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：土耳其语源敏感推理的基准测试：人类与大语言模型在证据信任操控下的表现
本文探讨土耳其语证据形态是否受信息来源可信度影响，以及大语言模型（LLMs）是否能追踪这种敏感性。我们在受控的填空语境中研究了过去域中 -DI 与 -mIs 的对比，其中信息来源明确为外部，但其感知可靠性被操控（高可信度 vs. 低可信度）。在人类生成实验中，土耳其语母语者表现出显著的信任效应：高可信度语境中 -DI 使用较多，低可信度语境中 -mIs 使用较多，且该模式在敏感性分析中保持稳定。随后我们评估了10个LLMs在三种提示范式（开放式填空、明确过去时填空、强制选择A/B）下的表现。LLM行为高度依赖模型和提示方式：部分模型表现出微弱或局部的信任一致变化，但整体效应不稳定，常被反转，且常被输出合规性问题和强基率后缀偏好所掩盖。结果为土耳其语证据性的信任/承诺基础理论提供了新证据，并揭示了人类与LLM在源敏感证据推理上的明显差距。

When tested with ten LLMs across three prompting methods, models showed inconsistent and often reversed trust-related shifts, frequently dominated by base-rate suffix preferences and output-compliance issues. The findings support a trust-commitment account of Turkish evidential morphology and highlight a significant gap between human and LLM performance in source-sensitive reasoning.
本研究考察土耳其语使用者和大语言模型（LLM）是否根据信息来源的可信度调整证据形态（-DI vs. -mIs）的使用。在控制实验中，母语者在高可信度情境下更倾向于使用-DI，低可信度情境下更倾向使用-mIs，该模式稳定且不受敏感性分析影响。相比之下，十种LLM在三种提示方式下表现不一，常出现与信任信号相反的倾向，且多受基础频率偏好的干扰，显示出人类与机器在来源敏感性证据推理上的显著差距。
Dual Control of Linear Systems from Bilinear Observations with Belief Space Model Predictive Control
Authors: Daniel Cao, Beixi Du, Andrew Lowitt, Sunmook Choi, Sarah Dean, Yahya Sattar
First: 2026-04-27T16:25:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:25:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study finite-horizon quadratic control of linear systems with bilinear observations, in which the control input affects not only the state dynamics but also the partial observations of the state. In this setting, the separation principle can fail because control inputs influence the future quality of state estimates. State estimation requires an input-dependent Kalman filter whose gain and error covariance evolve as functions of the control inputs. To address this challenge, we propose a belief-space model predictive control ($\texttt{B-MPC}$) method that plans directly over both the estimated state and its error covariance. In particular, $\texttt{B-MPC}$ plans with a deterministic surrogate of the belief evolution defined by the input-dependent Kalman filter. Through numerical experiments in two synthetic settings, we show that $\texttt{B-MPC}$ can outperform both the separation-principle controller and its MPC variant in favorable regimes, and that these gains are accompanied by lower estimation covariance and more uncertainty-aware action choices.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于信念空间模型预测控制的线性系统双控问题研究
我们研究了具有双线性观测的线性系统的有限时域二次控制问题，其中控制输入不仅影响系统状态动态，还影响状态的部分观测。在此设定下，分离原理可能失效，因为控制输入会影响未来状态估计的质量。状态估计需要依赖输入的卡尔曼滤波器，其增益和误差协方差随控制输入变化。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种信念空间模型预测控制（B-MPC）方法，直接在估计状态及其误差协方差上进行规划。具体而言，B-MPC使用输入依赖卡尔曼滤波器定义的确定性代理模型来规划信念演化。通过两个合成场景的数值实验，我们表明B-MPC在有利条件下可优于分离原理控制器及其MPC变体，且这些优势伴随着更低的估计协方差和更具有不确定感知性的动作选择。

This paper addresses the challenge of controlling linear systems with bilinear observations, where control inputs affect both state dynamics and observation quality, breaking the separation principle. The authors propose belief-space model predictive control (B-MPC), which directly plans over the estimated state and its error covariance using a deterministic surrogate of the input-dependent Kalman filter. Experimental results on two synthetic benchmarks demonstrate that B-MPC achieves better performance than both the standard separation-based controller and its MPC variant, while also reducing estimation covariance and producing more uncertainty-aware control actions.

Information bottleneck for learning the phase space of dynamics from high-dimensional experimental data
Authors: K. Michael Martini, Eslam Abdelaleem, Paarth Gulati, Ilya Nemenman
First: 2026-04-27T16:24:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:24:45+00:00
Comments: 12 pages including references, 7 figures, 4 appendix pages with 4 appendix figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Identifying the dynamical state variables of a system from high-dimensional observations is a central problem across physical sciences. The challenge is that the state variables are not directly observable and must be inferred from raw high-dimensional data without supervision. Here we introduce DySIB (Dynamical Symmetric Information Bottleneck) as a method to learn low-dimensional representations of time-series data by maximizing predictive mutual information between past and future observation windows while penalizing representation complexity. This objective operates entirely in latent space and avoids reconstruction of the observations. We apply DySIB to an experimental video dataset of a physical pendulum, where the underlying state space is known. The method, with hyperparameters of the learning architecture set self-consistently by the data, recovers a two-dimensional representation that matches the dimensionality, topology, and geometry of the pendulum phase space, with the learned coordinates aligning smoothly with the canonical angle and angular velocity. These results demonstrate, on a well-characterized experimental system, that predictive information in latent space can be used to recover interpretable dynamical coordinates directly from high-dimensional data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从高维实验数据中学习动力学相空间的信息瓶颈方法
从高维观测数据中识别系统的动力学状态变量是物理科学中的核心问题。挑战在于状态变量无法直接观测，必须在无监督条件下从原始高维数据中推断。本文提出DySIB（动力学对称信息瓶颈）方法，通过最大化过去与未来观测窗口之间的预测互信息，同时惩罚表示复杂度，学习时间序列数据的低维表示。该目标完全在潜在空间中操作，无需重建观测数据。我们将其应用于物理摆的实验视频数据集，其中底层状态空间已知。该方法通过数据自洽地设定学习架构超参数，恢复出与摆相空间维度、拓扑和几何结构一致的二维表示，所学坐标平滑对应于标准角度和角速度。这些结果表明，在一个已明确表征的实验系统中，潜在空间中的预测信息可直接从高维数据中恢复出可解释的动力学坐标。
Summary / 总结
Identifying the dynamical state variables of a system from high-dimensional observations is a central problem across physical sciences.
为从高维实验数据中无监督地推断隐藏的动力学状态变量，作者提出DySIB方法，通过最大化过去与未来观测窗口间的预测互信息并惩罚表示复杂度，学习低维表征。应用于已知相空间的物理摆视频数据集时，DySIB在自洽调整架构超参数后，恢复出与真实相空间维度、拓扑和几何结构一致的二维低维表征，其学习到的坐标与标准角度和角速度平滑对齐。
Agent-Centric Visual Reinforcement Learning under Dynamic Perturbations
Authors: Zhengru Fang, Yu Guo, Fei Liu, Yuang Zhang, Yihang Tao, Senkang Hu, Wenbo Ding, Yuguang Fang
First: 2026-04-27T16:24:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:24:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual reinforcement learning aims to empower an agent to learn policies from visual observations, yet it remains vulnerable to dynamic visual perturbations, such as unpredictable shifts in corruption types. To systematically study this, we introduce the Visual Degraded Control Suite (VDCS), a benchmark extending DeepMind Control Suite with Markov-switching degradations to simulate non-stationary real-world perturbations. Experiments on VDCS reveal severe performance degradation in existing methods. We theoretically prove via information-theoretic analysis that this failure stems from reconstruction-based objectives inevitably entangling perturbation artifacts into latent representations. To mitigate this negative impact, we propose Agent-Centric Observations with Mixture-of-Experts (ACO-MoE) to robustify visual RL against perturbations. The proposed framework leverages unique agent-centric restoration experts, achieving restoration from corruptions and task-relevant foreground extraction, thereby decoupling perception from perturbation before being processed by the RL agent. Extensive experiments on VDCS show our ACO-MoE outperforms strong baselines, recovering 95.3% of clean performance under challenging Markov-switching corruptions. Moreover, it achieves SOTA results on DMControl Generalization with random-color and video-background perturbations, demonstrating a high level of robustness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：动态扰动下的以代理为中心的视觉强化学习
视觉强化学习旨在使代理从视觉观测中学习策略，但其易受动态视觉扰动影响，如不可预测的损坏类型变化。为系统研究此问题，我们引入视觉退化控制套件（VDCS），该基准在DeepMind控制套件基础上扩展了马尔可夫切换退化，以模拟非平稳现实世界扰动。在VDCS上的实验表明现有方法性能严重下降。我们通过信息论分析理论证明，该失败源于基于重建的目标不可避免地将扰动伪影嵌入潜在表示中。为减轻此负面影响，我们提出以代理为中心的观测与专家混合（ACO-MoE），以增强视觉强化学习对扰动的鲁棒性。该框架利用独特的以代理为中心的恢复专家，实现从损坏中恢复及任务相关前景提取，从而在被强化学习代理处理前将感知与扰动解耦。在VDCS上的大量实验表明，ACO-MoE优于强基线方法，在具有挑战性的马尔可夫切换损坏下恢复95.3%的干净性能。此外，它在DMControl泛化任务中随机颜色和视频背景扰动下达到最先进水平，展现出高度鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the vulnerability of visual reinforcement learning to dynamic visual perturbations by introducing the Visual Degraded Control Suite (VDCS), a benchmark that simulates non-stationary corruptions via Markov-switching degradations. The authors demonstrate that existing methods suffer severe performance degradation under such conditions, theoretically attributing this to reconstruction-based objectives that inadvertently encode perturbation artifacts into latent representations. To resolve this, they propose ACO-MoE, a framework that employs agent-centric restoration experts to separate perturbation from task-relevant visual content before RL processing. Experiments on VDCS show ACO-MoE recovers 95.3% of clean performance under challenging Markov-switching corruptions and achieves state-of-the-art results on DMControl Generalization with random-color and video-background perturbations.
该论文针对视觉强化学习在动态视觉扰动下的脆弱性，提出视觉退化控制套件（VDCS）作为基准，通过马尔可夫切换退化模拟非平稳现实世界扰动。实验表明现有方法在该环境下性能严重下降，理论分析指出其根源在于重建型目标函数将扰动伪影与潜在表征混杂在一起。为此，作者提出ACO-MoE框架，利用以智能体为中心的修复专家分离扰动与任务相关视觉内容，再供强化学习模块处理。在VDCS上的实验显示，ACO-MoE在挑战性马尔可夫切换扰动下恢复95.3%的干净性能，并在DMControl泛化任务中于随机颜色与视频背景扰动下达到最先进水平。
Conjecture and Inquiry: Quantifying Software Performance Requirements via Interactive Retrieval-Augmented Preference Elicitation
Authors: Shihai Wang, Tao Chen
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-23T07:50:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:24:25+00:00
Comments: 9 pages,accepted by ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Since software performance requirements are documented in natural language, quantifying them into mathematical forms is essential for software engineering. Yet, the vagueness in performance requirements and uncertainty of human cognition have caused highly uncertain ambiguity in the interpretations, rendering their automated quantification an unaddressed and challenging problem. In this paper, we formalize the problem and propose IRAP, an approach that quantifies performance requirements into mathematical functions via interactive retrieval-augmented preference elicitation. IRAP differs from the others in that it explicitly derives from problem-specific knowledge to retrieve and reason the preferences, which also guides the progressive interaction with stakeholders, while reducing the cognitive overhead. Experiment results against 10 state-of-the-art methods on four real-world datasets demonstrate the superiority of IRAP on all cases with up to 40x improvements under as few as five rounds of interactions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：猜想与探究：通过交互式检索增强偏好获取量化软件性能需求
由于软件性能需求以自然语言记录，将其量化为数学形式对软件工程至关重要。然而，性能需求的模糊性与人类认知的不确定性导致其解释高度模糊，使其自动化量化成为一个未解决且具有挑战性的问题。本文形式化该问题，并提出IRAP方法，通过交互式检索增强偏好获取将性能需求量化为数学函数。IRAP不同于其他方法之处在于，它从问题特定知识出发，检索并推理偏好，同时指导与利益相关者的渐进式交互，降低认知负担。在四个真实数据集上与10种最新方法对比实验表明，IRAP在所有情况下均表现优越，交互轮次少至五轮时性能提升最高可达40倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to convert natural-language software performance requirements into precise mathematical forms, this paper introduces IRAP, an interactive retrieval-augmented preference elicitation method that leverages problem-specific knowledge to guide stakeholder interactions and reduce cognitive load. By retrieving relevant context and reasoning over preferences iteratively, IRAP progressively refines performance requirements into quantifiable functions. Experiments across four real-world datasets against ten state-of-the-art methods show IRAP consistently outperforms alternatives, achieving up to 40x improvement in accuracy with as few as five interaction rounds.
为解决软件性能需求从自然语言到数学形式的量化难题，本文提出IRAP方法，通过结合领域知识的检索增强交互式偏好获取，逐步引导利益相关者交互以降低认知负担。该方法利用检索上下文动态调整性能约束的数学模型。在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，IRAP在仅五轮交互内即可实现比十种现有方法高40倍的准确率。
The Last Human-Written Paper: Agent-Native Research Artifacts
Authors: Jiachen Liu, Jiaxin Pei, Jintao Huang, Chenglei Si, Ao Qu, Xiangru Tang, Runyu Lu, Lichang Chen, Xiaoyan Bai, Haizhong Zheng, Carl Chen, Zhiyang Chen, Haojie Ye, Yujuan Fu, Zexue He, Zijian Jin, Zhenyu Zhang, Shangquan Sun, Maestro Harmon, John Dianzhuo Wang, Jianqiao Zeng, Jiachen Sun, Mingyuan Wu, Baoyu Zhou, Yuchen You, Shijian Lu, Yiming Qiu, Fan Lai, Yuan Yuan, Yao Li, Junyuan Hong, Ruihao Zhu, Beidi Chen, Alex Pentland, Ang Chen, Mosharaf Chowdhury, Zechen Zhang
First: 2026-04-27T16:23:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:23:09+00:00
Comments: 45 pages, 15 figures, 14 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific publication compresses a branching, iterative research process into a linear narrative, discarding the majority of what was discovered along the way. This compilation imposes two structural costs: a Storytelling Tax, where failed experiments, rejected hypotheses, and the branching exploration process are discarded to fit a linear narrative; and an Engineering Tax, where the gap between reviewer-sufficient prose and agent-sufficient specification leaves critical implementation details unwritten. Tolerable for human readers, these costs become critical when AI agents must understand, reproduce, and extend published work. We introduce the Agent-Native Research Artifact (Ara), a protocol that replaces the narrative paper with a machine-executable research package structured around four layers: scientific logic, executable code with full specifications, an exploration graph that preserves the failures compilation discards, and evidence grounding every claim in raw outputs. Three mechanisms support the ecosystem: a Live Research Manager that captures decisions and dead ends during ordinary development; an Ara Compiler that translates legacy PDFs and repos into Aras; and an Ara-native review system that automates objective checks so human reviewers can focus on significance, novelty, and taste. On PaperBench and RE-Bench, Ara raises question-answering accuracy from 72.4% to 93.7% and reproduction success from 57.4% to 64.4%. On RE-Bench's five open-ended extension tasks, preserved failure traces in Ara accelerate progress, but can also constrain a capable agent from stepping outside the prior-run box depending on the agent's capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：最后一篇人工撰写的论文：代理原生研究产物
科学出版物将分支迭代的研究过程压缩为线性叙事，舍弃了沿途发现的大部分内容。这种编纂带来两种结构性代价：叙事税，即为适应线性叙事而舍弃失败实验、被拒假设及分支探索过程；工程税，即评审者可理解的文本与代理可执行的规范之间存在差距，导致关键实现细节未被书写。对人类读者尚可容忍，但当AI代理必须理解、复现并扩展已发表工作时，这些代价变得至关重要。我们提出代理原生研究产物（Ara），一种替代叙事论文的机器可执行研究包，围绕四个层次构建：科学逻辑、带完整规范的可执行代码、保留失败记录的探索图谱，以及将每项主张锚定在原始输出证据上的机制。生态系统由三部分机制支持：实时研究管理器，用于捕获普通开发过程中的决策与死胡同；Ara编译器，将旧PDF和代码库转换为Ara格式；以及原生Ara评审系统，自动化客观检查，使人类评审者聚焦于重要性、新颖性与品味。在PaperBench和RE-Bench上，Ara将问答准确率从72.4%提升至93.7%，复现成功率从57.4%提升至64.4%。在RE-Bench的五个开放式扩展任务中，Ara保留的失败轨迹加速了进展，但根据代理能力不同，也可能限制其超出前次运行范围。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional scientific papers in conveying full research processes to AI agents, this work proposes the Agent-Native Research Artifact (Ara), a machine-executable format structured into four layers: scientific logic, executable code with full specs, an exploration graph capturing failures, and evidence grounding. The system includes tools to capture development decisions, convert legacy materials into Aras, and automate review. Experiments on PaperBench and RE-Bench show Ara boosts question-answering accuracy from 72.4% to 93.7% and reproduction success from 57.4% to 64.4%, while preserved failure traces aid extension tasks but may also constrain agent behavior depending on capability.
为解决传统科研论文难以完整传达研究过程给AI代理的问题，本文提出Agent-Native Research Artifact（Ara）——一种机器可执行的结构化研究包，包含科学逻辑、带完整规格的可执行代码、记录失败路径的探索图谱以及原始输出证据锚定。系统配套工具包括Live Research Manager（记录开发决策）、Ara Compiler（将旧格式材料转为Ara）和Ara原生评审系统（自动化客观检查）。实验显示，Ara在PaperBench和RE-Bench上将问答准确率从72.4%提升至93.7%，复现成功率从57.4%提升至64.4%，且保留的失败轨迹可加速扩展任务，但可能因代理能力限制而约束其突破原有框架。
AgentWard: A Lifecycle Security Architecture for Autonomous AI Agents
Authors: Yixiang Zhang, Xinhao Deng, Jiaqing Wu, Yue Xiao, Ke Xu, Qi Li
First: 2026-04-27T16:22:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:22:27+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 1 figure;
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Autonomous AI agents extend large language models into full runtime systems that load skills, ingest external content, maintain memory, plan multi-step actions, and invoke privileged tools. In such systems, security failures rarely remain confined to a single interface; instead, they can propagate across initialization, input processing, memory, decision-making, and execution, often becoming apparent only when harmful effects materialize in the environment. This paper presents AgentWard, a lifecycle-oriented, defense-in-depth architecture that systematically organizes protection across these five stages. AgentWard integrates stage-specific, heterogeneous controls with cross-layer coordination, enabling threats to be intercepted along their propagation paths while safeguarding critical assets. We detail the design rationale and architecture of five coordinated protection layers, and implement a plugin-native prototype on OpenClaw to demonstrate practical feasibility. This perspective provides a concrete blueprint for structuring runtime security controls, managing trust propagation, and enforcing execution containment in autonomous AI agents. Our code is available at https://github.com/FIND-Lab/AgentWard .
中文标题/摘要
标题：AgentWard：面向自主AI代理的生命周期安全架构
自主AI代理将大语言模型扩展为完整的运行时系统，可加载技能、摄取外部内容、维护记忆、规划多步骤操作并调用特权工具。在这些系统中，安全失效很少局限于单一接口；相反，它们可能在初始化、输入处理、内存、决策和执行等阶段传播，往往直到有害影响在环境中显现时才被发现。本文提出AgentWard，一种面向生命周期、纵深防御的架构，系统性地在上述五个阶段组织保护措施。AgentWard整合各阶段特定的异构控制机制与跨层协调，使威胁能在传播路径中被拦截，同时保护关键资产。我们详细阐述了五个协调保护层的设计原理与架构，并在OpenClaw上实现了一个插件原生原型，以证明其实际可行性。本文提供了一个具体蓝图，用于构建运行时安全控制、管理信任传播和强制执行执行隔离，适用于自主AI代理。代码开源于https://github.com/FIND-Lab/AgentWard。

Autonomous AI agents extend large language models into full runtime systems that load skills, ingest external content, maintain memory, plan multi-step actions, and invoke privileged tools.
Gradient-Guided Exploration of Generative Model's Latent Space for Controlled Iris Image Augmentations
Authors: Mahsa Mitcheff, Siamul Karim Khan, Adam Czajka
First: 2025-11-12T21:26:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:19:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Developing reliable iris recognition and presentation attack detection methods requires diverse datasets that capture realistic variations in iris features and a wide spectrum of anomalies. Because of the rich texture of iris images, which spans a wide range of spatial frequencies, synthesizing same-identity iris images while controlling specific attributes remains challenging. In this work, we introduce a new iris image augmentation strategy by traversing a generative model's latent space toward latent codes that represent same-identity samples but with some desired iris image properties manipulated. The latent space traversal is guided by a gradient of specific geometrical, textural, or quality-related iris image features (e.g., sharpness, pupil size, iris size, or pupil-to-iris ratio) and preserves the identity represented by the image being manipulated. The proposed approach can be easily extended to manipulate any attribute for which a differentiable loss term can be formulated. Additionally, our approach can use either randomly generated images using either a pre-train GAN model or real-world iris images. We can utilize GAN inversion to project any given iris image into the latent space and obtain its corresponding latent code.

标题：基于梯度引导的生成模型潜在空间探索用于可控虹膜图像增强
开发可靠的虹膜识别与呈现攻击检测方法需要多样化的数据集，以捕捉虹膜特征的现实变化及广泛异常。由于虹膜图像纹理丰富，涵盖广泛的空域频率，合成相同身份但控制特定属性的虹膜图像仍具挑战性。本文提出一种新的虹膜图像增强策略：通过在生成模型的潜在空间中沿特定几何、纹理或质量相关虹膜图像特征（如清晰度、瞳孔大小、虹膜大小或瞳孔-虹膜比）的梯度方向进行遍历，生成相同身份但具有期望属性的虹膜图像。该方法在操作过程中保持图像所代表的身份。该方法可轻松扩展至任何可构建可微分损失项的属性。此外，该方法可使用预训练GAN模型生成的随机图像或真实虹膜图像。我们可利用GAN反演将任意给定虹膜图像投影至潜在空间，获取其对应的潜在编码。

Computational Design and Co-Robotic Fabrication for Material Reuse in Architecture
Authors: Arash Adel, Daniel Ruan, Ruxin Xie
First: 2026-04-27T16:16:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:16:15+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in Proceedings of the 45th Annual Conference of the Association for Computer Aided Design in Architecture (ACADIA 2025)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Climate change and resource depletion demand a shift from the dominant linear "take-make-use-dispose" paradigm of construction toward circular, low-waste practices. Material reuse offers a promising pathway by reducing raw material extraction, mitigating waste, and extending the service lifespan of carbon-sequestering materials such as timber. Realizing this potential, however, requires addressing technical and logistical challenges across both design and construction for accommodating heterogeneous, reclaimed material inventories. This paper presents an integrated framework that couples data-driven computational design with feedback-driven adaptive human-robot collaborative (co-robotic) fabrication and assembly to enable the realization of nonstandard structures made from reclaimed timber of varying length and geometries, supplemented with new off-the-shelf timber when necessary. The framework is validated through Timbrelyn, a built case-study installation that demonstrates how timber reuse can inform and enhance architectural expression. This work contributes to the development of integrated design-to-fabrication workflows that advance adaptive, feedback-driven methods to handle inventory constraints and reclaimed material uncertainties, facilitating material reuse in the design and construction of new buildings and structures.

标题：面向建筑领域材料再利用的计算设计与人机协作制造
气候变化和资源枯竭要求建筑行业从主流的线性“取-制-用-弃”模式转向循环、低废的实践方式。材料再利用通过减少原材料开采、降低废弃物、延长碳封存材料（如木材）的使用寿命，提供了一条有前景的路径。然而，要实现这一潜力，需解决设计与施工过程中针对异质性回收材料库存的技术与物流挑战。 本文提出一个集成框架，将数据驱动的计算设计与反馈驱动的自适应人机协作（共机器人）制造与装配相结合，以实现由不同长度和几何形态的回收木材构成的非标准结构，并在必要时辅以现成木材。该框架通过Timbrelyn建成案例研究装置得到验证，展示了木材再利用如何影响并增强建筑表达。本研究推动了从设计到制造的集成工作流程的发展，促进适应性、反馈驱动的方法应对库存约束与回收材料不确定性，从而支持新建建筑与结构中的材料再利用。

Driven by the urgent need to transition construction from a linear to a circular model, this research develops a computational design and co-robotic fabrication framework to enable the use of heterogeneous reclaimed timber. The method integrates data-driven design with adaptive human-robot collaboration, allowing for the assembly of nonstandard structures using variable-length reclaimed materials, supplemented by new timber when needed. Experimental validation through the Timbrelyn installation demonstrates the framework’s ability to handle material uncertainties and inventory constraints while achieving architectural expression through reuse.
为应对气候变化与资源枯竭，本研究提出一套计算设计与人机协作制造框架，以实现异质再生木材在建筑结构中的应用。该方法结合数据驱动设计与反馈式人机协同制造，可实时调整加工过程以适应不同尺寸与形状的再生木材，并在必要时补充现成木材。通过Timbrelyn实际建造案例验证，该框架成功实现了非标准木材构件的组装，证明其能有效应对材料不确定性，同时提升建筑表达力。
DepthKV: Layer-Dependent KV Cache Pruning for Long-Context LLM Inference
Authors: Zahra Dehghanighobadi, Asja Fischer
First: 2026-04-27T16:15:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:15:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-context reasoning is a critical capability of large language models (LLMs), enabling applications such as long-document understanding, summarization, and code generation. However, efficient autoregressive inference relies on the key-value (KV) cache, whose memory footprint grows linearly with sequence length, leading to a major memory bottleneck. To mitigate this overhead, KV cache pruning methods discard cached tokens with low attention scores during inference. Most existing methods apply a uniform pruning ratio across layers, implicitly assuming that all layers contribute equally to overall model performance. We show that this assumption is suboptimal, as layers differ significantly in their sensitivity to pruning. We propose DepthKV, a layer-dependent pruning framework that allocates a fixed global KV budget across layers based on their sensitivity, rather than using a uniform allocation. Across multiple models and tasks, DepthKV consistently outperforms uniform pruning at the same global pruning ratio, demonstrating more effective utilization of the KV cache budget through layer-dependent allocation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DepthKV：面向长上下文LLM推理的层依赖KV缓存剪枝
长上下文推理是大型语言模型（LLM）的关键能力，支持长文档理解、摘要生成和代码生成等应用。然而，高效的自回归推理依赖于键值（KV）缓存，其内存开销随序列长度线性增长，造成主要内存瓶颈。为缓解此开销，KV缓存剪枝方法在推理过程中丢弃注意力分数低的缓存token。现有方法普遍采用统一剪枝比例跨层应用，隐含假设各层对整体模型性能贡献相等。我们证明该假设次优，因各层对剪枝的敏感度差异显著。我们提出DepthKV，一种层依赖剪枝框架，根据各层敏感度分配固定全局KV预算，而非统一分配。在多个模型与任务中，DepthKV在相同全局剪枝比例下持续优于统一剪枝，通过层依赖分配更有效利用KV缓存预算。
Summary / 总结
To address the memory bottleneck caused by growing KV cache sizes in long-context LLM inference, the paper proposes DepthKV, a layer-dependent KV cache pruning method that allocates a fixed global pruning budget based on each layer’s sensitivity to pruning, rather than applying uniform pruning across all layers. Experimental results across multiple models and tasks show that DepthKV consistently outperforms uniform pruning at the same global pruning ratio, demonstrating more efficient use of the KV cache budget by adapting to layer-specific importance.
为缓解长上下文推理中KV缓存随序列长度线性增长导致的内存瓶颈，本文提出DepthKV方法，根据各层对剪枝的敏感度动态分配固定全局剪枝预算，而非对所有层采用统一剪枝比例。实验表明，不同层对剪枝的敏感度差异显著，按敏感度分配预算可更高效利用缓存资源。在多个模型和任务上，DepthKV在相同全局剪枝比例下始终优于统一剪枝方法，验证了其有效性。
Complementarity by Construction: A Lie-Group Approach to Solving Quadratic Programs with Linear Complementarity Constraints
Authors: Arun L. Bishop, Micah I. Reich, Zachary Manchester
First: 2026-04-13T19:31:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:14:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many problems in robotics require reasoning over a mix of continuous dynamics and discrete events, such as making and breaking contact in manipulation and locomotion. These problems are locally well modeled by linear complementarity quadratic programs (LCQPs), an extension to QPs that introduce complementarity constraints. While very expressive, LCQPs are non-convex, and few solvers exist for computing good local solutions for use in planning pipelines. In this work, we observe that complementarity constraints form a Lie group under infinitesimal relaxation, and leverage this structure to perform on-manifold optimization. We introduce a retraction map that is numerically well behaved, and use it to parameterize the constraints so that they are satisfied by construction. The resulting solver avoids many of the classical issues with complementarity constraints. We provide an open-source solver, Marble, that is implemented in C++ with Julia and Python bindings. We demonstrate that Marble is competitive on a suite of benchmark problems, and solves a number of robotics problems where existing approaches fail to converge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：构造互补性：一种利用李群方法求解带线性互补约束的二次规划问题
机器人领域的许多问题需要在连续动力学与离散事件之间进行推理，例如操作与运动中的接触建立与解除。这些问题在局部可被线性互补二次规划（LCQPs）很好地建模，这是对二次规划引入互补约束的扩展。尽管LCQPs表达能力很强，但它们是非凸的，且现有求解器极少能有效计算局部良好解用于规划流程。本文观察到，互补约束在微小松弛下构成李群，并利用该结构进行流形上的优化。我们引入一个数值表现良好的重映射，用其参数化约束，使约束在构造上自动满足。该求解器避免了互补约束的许多经典问题。我们提供了一个开源求解器Marble，用C++实现并支持Julia和Python接口。我们证明Marble在一系列基准问题上表现优异，并能解决现有方法无法收敛的若干机器人问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model hybrid systems in robotics involving continuous dynamics and discrete contact events, this work addresses the non-convex optimization challenges posed by linear complementarity quadratic programs (LCQPs). It leverages the Lie group structure inherent in complementarity constraints under infinitesimal relaxation, introducing a numerically stable retraction map to enforce constraints on-manifold during optimization. This approach yields a solver, Marble, that avoids classical convergence issues and demonstrates competitive performance on benchmark problems, successfully solving robotics tasks where prior methods fail to converge.
为解决机器人中连续动力学与离散事件（如接触）混合建模所引发的非凸优化难题，该研究利用互补约束在微小松弛下的李群结构，引入数值稳定的重映射方法，使约束在优化过程中自动满足。由此构建的求解器Marble避免了传统互补约束的收敛问题，在基准测试中表现优异，并成功解决了现有方法无法收敛的机器人任务。
K-MetBench: A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Fine-Grained Evaluation of Expert Reasoning, Locality, and Multimodality in Meteorology
Authors: Soyeon Kim, Cheongwoong Kang, Myeongjin Lee, Eun-Chul Chang, Jaedeok Lee, Jaesik Choi
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-27T16:13:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:13:14+00:00
Comments: 39 pages, 32 figures, 14 tables, including appendices. Accepted to Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics (ACL 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The development of practical (multimodal) large language model assistants for Korean weather forecasters is hindered by the absence of a multidimensional, expert-level evaluation framework grounded in authoritative sources. To address this, we introduce K-MetBench, a diagnostic benchmark grounded in national qualification exams. It exposes critical gaps across four dimensions: expert visual reasoning of charts, logical validity via expert-verified rationales, Korean-specific geo-cultural comprehension, and fine-grained domain analysis. Our evaluation of 55 models reveals a profound modality gap in interpreting specialized diagrams and a reasoning gap where models hallucinate logic despite correct predictions. Crucially, Korean models outperform significantly larger global models in local contexts, demonstrating that parameter scaling alone cannot resolve cultural dependencies. K-MetBench serves as a roadmap for developing reliable, culturally aware expert AI agents. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/soyeonbot/K-MetBench .

韩国气象预报员实用（多模态）大语言模型助手的发展受限于缺乏基于权威来源的多维专家级评估框架。为此，我们引入K-MetBench，该基准基于国家资格考试构建，揭示了四个维度的关键差距：专家图表视觉推理、经专家验证的逻辑有效性、韩语特定的地理文化理解及细粒度领域分析。对55个模型的评估显示，模型在解读专业图表时存在显著模态差距，且即使预测正确也常虚构逻辑推理。重要的是，韩国本地模型在本地语境下显著优于参数规模更大的全球模型，表明单纯参数扩展无法解决文化依赖问题。K-MetBench为开发可靠且文化敏感的专家AI代理提供了路线图。数据集可在https://huggingface.co/datasets/soyeonbot/K-MetBench获取。

To address the lack of a multidimensional evaluation framework for Korean weather forecasting assistants, K-MetBench was developed using national qualification exam materials to assess expert reasoning, visual interpretation, cultural context, and multimodal analysis. Analyzing Chain of Thought (CoT) Approaches in Control Flow Code Deobfuscation Tasks
Authors: Seyedreza Mohseni, Sarvesh Baskar, Edward Raff, Manas Gaur
First: 2026-04-16T06:08:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:12:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Code deobfuscation is the task of recovering a readable version of a program while preserving its original behavior. In practice, this often requires days or even months of manual work with complex and expensive analysis tools. In this paper, we explore an alternative approach based on Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting, where a large language model is guided through explicit, step-by-step reasoning tailored for code analysis. We focus on control flow obfuscation, including Control Flow Flattening (CFF), Opaque Predicates, and their combination, and we measure both structural recovery of the control flow graph and preservation of program semantics. We evaluate five state-of-the-art large language models and show that CoT prompting significantly improves deobfuscation quality compared with simple prompting. We validate our approach on a diverse set of standard C benchmarks and report results using both structural metrics for control flow graphs and semantic metrics based on output similarity. Among the tested models and by applying CoT, GPT5 achieves the strongest overall performance, with an average gain of about 16% in control-flow graph reconstruction and about 20.5% in semantic preservation across our benchmarks compared to zero-shot prompting. Our results also show that model performance depends not only on the obfuscation level and the chosen obfuscator but also on the intrinsic complexity of the original control flow graph. Collectively, these findings suggest that CoT-guided large language models can serve as effective assistants for code deobfuscation, providing improved code explainability, more faithful control flow graph reconstruction, and better preservation of program behavior while potentially reducing the manual effort needed for reverse engineering.
中文标题/摘要
标题：控制流代码反混淆任务中链式思维（CoT）方法的分析
代码反混淆旨在恢复程序的可读版本，同时保持其原始行为。实际操作中，这通常需要数天甚至数月的人工工作及复杂昂贵的分析工具。本文探索了一种基于链式思维（CoT）提示的替代方法，通过引导大型语言模型进行针对代码分析的显式、分步推理。我们聚焦于控制流混淆，包括控制流扁平化（CFF）、不透明谓词及其组合，并评估控制流图结构恢复与程序语义保持的效果。我们评估了五种最先进的大型语言模型，结果表明CoT提示显著优于简单提示的反混淆质量。我们在多样化的标准C基准上验证了该方法，并使用控制流图结构指标和基于输出相似度的语义指标报告结果。在测试模型中，应用CoT后GPT5表现最佳，相比零样本提示，其控制流图重建平均提升约16%，语义保持平均提升约20.5%。我们的结果还表明，模型性能不仅取决于混淆程度和所选混淆器，还与原始控制流图的内在复杂性相关。综合来看，这些发现表明，CoT引导的大型语言模型可作为代码反混淆的有效助手，提供更优的代码可解释性、更忠实的控制流图重建及更好的程序行为保持，同时可能减少逆向工程所需的人工工作量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high manual effort and cost of traditional code deobfuscation, this study investigates Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting as a method to guide large language models through step-by-step reasoning for control flow deobfuscation. The approach targets obfuscation techniques like Control Flow Flattening and Opaque Predicates, evaluating both structural recovery of control flow graphs and semantic preservation. Experiments across five LLMs on diverse C benchmarks show that CoT significantly outperforms simple prompting, with GPT5 achieving the best results: a 16% average improvement in control-flow graph reconstruction and 20.5% in semantic preservation. Performance also varies by obfuscation type and original control flow complexity, indicating CoT’s effectiveness as a tool to enhance reverse engineering efficiency and code interpretability.

Probing CLIP's Comprehension of 360-Degree Textual and Visual Semantics
Authors: Hai Wang, Xiaochen Yang, Mingzhi Dong, Jing-Hao Xue
First: 2026-04-27T16:10:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:10:00+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://littlewhitesea.github.io/360Semantics.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
The dream of instantly creating rich 360-degree panoramic worlds from text is rapidly becoming a reality, yet a crucial gap exists in our ability to reliably evaluate their semantic alignment. Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) models, standard AI evaluators, predominantly trained on perspective image-text pairs, face an open question regarding their understanding of the unique characteristics of 360-degree panoramic image-text pairs. This paper addresses this gap by first introducing two concepts: \emph{360-degree textual semantics}, semantic information conveyed by explicit format identifiers, and \emph{360-degree visual semantics}, invariant semantics under horizontal circular shifts. To probe CLIP's comprehension of these semantics, we then propose novel evaluation methodologies using keyword manipulation and horizontal circular shifts of varying magnitudes. Rigorous statistical analyses across popular CLIP configurations reveal that: (1) CLIP models effectively leverage explicit textual identifiers, demonstrating an understanding of 360-degree textual semantics; and (2) CLIP models fail to robustly preserve semantic alignment under horizontal circular shifts, indicating limited comprehension of 360-degree visual semantics. To address this limitation, we propose a LoRA-based fine-tuning framework that explicitly instills invariance to circular shifts. Our fine-tuned models exhibit improved comprehension of 360-degree visual semantics, though with a slight degradation in original semantic evaluation performance, highlighting a fundamental trade-off in adapting CLIP to 360-degree panoramic images. Code is available at https://github.com/littlewhitesea/360Semantics.

标题：探究CLIP对360度文本与视觉语义的理解
从文本即时生成丰富360度全景世界的梦想正迅速成为现实，但我们在可靠评估其语义对齐方面仍存在关键缺口。对比语言-图像预训练（CLIP）模型作为标准AI评估器，主要在透视图像-文本对上训练，其对360度全景图像-文本对独特特性的理解仍存疑问。本文通过引入两个概念解决此问题：\emph{360度文本语义}（由显式格式标识符传达的语义信息）和\emph{360度视觉语义}（在水平循环移位下保持不变的语义）。为探测CLIP对这些语义的理解，我们提出基于关键词操纵和不同幅度水平循环移位的新型评估方法。对主流CLIP配置的严谨统计分析表明：(1) CLIP模型有效利用显式文本标识符，表明其理解360度文本语义；(2) CLIP模型在水平循环移位下无法稳健保持语义对齐，表明其对360度视觉语义理解有限。为解决此局限，我们提出基于LoRA的微调框架，显式注入对循环移位的不变性。微调模型在360度视觉语义理解上表现提升，但原语义评估性能略有下降，凸显了将CLIP适配至360度全景图像时存在的根本权衡。代码见https://github.com/littlewhitesea/360Semantics。

This paper investigates CLIP's ability to understand 360-degree panoramic image-text pairs, motivated by the growing need to evaluate semantic alignment in immersive environments. 该研究旨在评估CLIP模型对360度全景图像-文本对语义对齐的理解能力，以应对沉浸式环境中的评价需求。作者提出两个核心概念：通过显式格式标识符传达的360度文本语义，以及在水平循环平移下保持不变的360度视觉语义。通过关键词操纵和不同幅度的循环平移实验，作者发现CLIP能有效利用文本标识符，但在视觉平移下难以保持语义一致性，表明其对视觉语义理解不足。为此，作者提出基于LoRA的微调框架，强制模型学习循环平移不变性，虽提升了视觉语义理解能力，但牺牲了原有语义评估性能，揭示了适配360度内容时的性能权衡。
Beyond ReLU: How Activations Affect Neural Kernels and Random Wide Networks
Authors: David Holzmüller, Max Schölpple
First: 2025-06-27T17:56:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:08:52+00:00
Comments: Published at AISTATS 2026. New in v2: more discussions, plots on empirical eigenvalue decay
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recent years, the neural tangent kernel (NTK) and neural network Gaussian process kernel (NNGP) have given theoreticians tractable limiting cases of fully connected neural networks. However, the property of these kernels are poorly understood for activation functions other than powers of the ReLU. Our main contribution is a characterization of the RKHS of these kernels for activation functions whose only non-smoothness is at zero. This extends existing theory to numerous commonly used activation functions such as SELU, ELU, or LeakyReLU. Additionally, we analyze a broad set of special cases such as missing biases, two-layer networks, or polynomial activations. Our results show that a broad class of not infinitely smooth activations generate equivalent RKHSs at different network depths, depending only on the degree of the non-smoothness up to equivalence. On the other hand, the RKHS generated by polynomial activations depends on the network depth. Finally, we derive results for the smoothness of NNGP sample paths, characterizing the smoothness of infinitely wide neural networks at initialization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越ReLU：激活函数如何影响神经核与随机宽网络
近年来，神经切线核（NTK）和神经网络高斯过程核（NNGP）为全连接神经网络提供了可处理的极限情况。然而，对于除ReLU幂次以外的激活函数，这些核的性质尚不明确。本文主要贡献是对仅在零点不光滑的激活函数所生成核的再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）进行刻画，从而将现有理论扩展至SELU、ELU或LeakyReLU等常用激活函数。此外，我们分析了多种特殊情况，如无偏置、两层网络或多项式激活函数。结果表明，一类非无限光滑激活函数在不同网络深度下生成等价的RKHS，其等价性仅取决于非光滑度的阶数。另一方面，多项式激活函数生成的RKHS依赖于网络深度。最后，我们推导了NNGP样本路径的光滑性结果，刻画了无限宽神经网络在初始化时的光滑性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited theoretical understanding of neural kernels beyond ReLU activations, this paper characterizes the reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHS) for a broad class of activation functions that are non-smooth only at zero, such as SELU, ELU, and LeakyReLU. Using analytical methods, the authors show that for many such activations, the RKHS remains equivalent across different network depths, depending only on the degree of non-smoothness. In contrast, polynomial activations yield RKHSs that depend on depth. The study also derives conditions for the smoothness of NNGP sample paths in infinitely wide networks, providing a theoretical foundation for understanding initialization behavior.
为弥补除ReLU幂函数外激活函数在神经核理论上的研究空白，本文对一类仅在零点不光滑的激活函数（如SELU、ELU、LeakyReLU）的再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）结构进行了刻画。研究发现，多数此类非光滑激活函数在不同网络深度下生成等价的RKHS，其差异仅取决于非光滑程度；而多项式激活函数的RKHS则依赖于网络深度。此外，论文还分析了NNGP样本路径在初始化时的光滑性，揭示了激活函数光滑性对无限宽神经网络行为的影响。
Cortex-Inspired Continual Learning: Unsupervised Instantiation and Recovery of Functional Task Networks
Authors: Kevin McKee, Thomas Hazy, Yicong Zheng, Zacharie Bugaud, Thomas Miconi
First: 2026-04-27T16:06:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:06:28+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 15 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Block-sequential continual learning demands that a single model both protect prior solutions from catastrophic forgetting and efficiently infer at inference time which prior solution matches the current input without task labels. We present Functional Task Networks (FTN), a parameter-isolation method inspired by structural and dynamical motifs found in the mammalian neocortex. Similar to mixture-of-experts, this method uses a high dimensional, self-organizing binary mask over a large population of small but deep networks, inspired by dendritic models of pyramidal neurons. The mask is produced by a three-stage procedure: (1) gradient descent on a continuous mask identifies task-relevant neurons, (2) a smoothing kernel biases the result toward spatial contiguity, (3) and k-winner-take-all binarizes the resulting group at a fixed capacity budget. Like mixture-of-experts, each neuron is an independent deep network, so disjoint masks give exactly disjoint gradient updates, providing structural guarantees against catastrophic forgetting. This three-stage procedure recovers the sub-network of a previously-trained task in a single gradient step, providing unsupervised task segmentation at inference time. We test it on three continual-learning benchmarks: (1) a synthetic multi-task classification/regression generator, (2) MNIST with shuffled class labels (pure concept shift), and (3) Permuted MNIST (domain shift). On all three, FTN with fine grained smoothing (FTN-Slow) results in nearly zero forgetting. FTN with a large kernel and only 2 iterations of smoothing (FTN-Fast) trades off some retention for increased speed. We show that the spatial organization mechanism reduces the effective mask search from the combinatorial top-k subset problem in O(C(H,K)) to the complexity of a near-linear scan in O(H) over compact cortical neighborhoods, which is parallelized by the gradient-based update.
中文标题/摘要
标题：受皮层启发的持续学习：无监督功能任务网络的实例化与恢复
块序列持续学习要求单个模型在保护先前解免遭灾难性遗忘的同时，在推理时高效推断当前输入与哪个先前解匹配（无需任务标签）。我们提出功能任务网络（FTN），这是一种受哺乳动物新皮层结构与动态模式启发的参数隔离方法。类似专家混合模型，该方法使用高维自组织二进制掩码覆盖大量小型但深度的网络，灵感来源于锥体神经元的树突模型。掩码通过三阶段过程生成：（1）在连续掩码上进行梯度下降以识别任务相关神经元；（2）平滑核使结果偏向空间连续性；（3）k-胜者取所有机制在固定容量预算下二值化所得组。与专家混合模型类似，每个神经元是独立的深度网络，因此不相交的掩码提供完全不相交的梯度更新，结构上保障免遭灾难性遗忘。该三阶段过程可在单次梯度步骤中恢复先前训练任务的子网络，实现推理时无监督任务分割。我们在三个持续学习基准上测试：（1）合成多任务分类/回归生成器；（2）标签打乱的MNIST（纯概念偏移）；（3）置换MNIST（域偏移）。在所有基准中，细粒度平滑的FTN（FTN-Slow）几乎无遗忘；而大核仅两轮平滑的FTN（FTN-Fast）以牺牲部分保留换取速度提升。我们证明，空间组织机制将掩码搜索复杂度从组合式top-k子集问题O(C(H,K))降低至近线性扫描O(H)在紧凑皮层邻域内，且梯度更新可并行化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to prevent catastrophic forgetting while enabling unsupervised task recovery in continual learning, the paper introduces Functional Task Networks (FTN), a parameter-isolation method inspired by cortical neuron dynamics. FTN employs a three-stage mask generation process: gradient descent identifies task-relevant neurons, a smoothing kernel enforces spatial contiguity, and k-winner-take-all binarization selects a fixed-capacity sub-network. Each neuron operates as an independent deep network, ensuring disjoint gradient updates and structural protection against forgetting. Experiments on three benchmarks—synthetic multi-task data, shuffled MNIST, and permuted MNIST—show that FTN-Slow achieves nearly zero forgetting, while FTN-Fast trades some retention for speed. The spatial organization mechanism reduces mask search complexity from combinatorial to near-linear, enabling efficient parallelization via gradient updates.
为解决持续学习中防止灾难性遗忘并实现无监督任务恢复的需求，本文提出受大脑皮层结构启发的Functional Task Networks（FTN）方法。该方法通过三阶段掩码生成：梯度下降识别任务相关神经元，平滑核强制空间连续性，k胜者取全二值化选择固定容量子网络。每个神经元独立运行深度网络，确保梯度更新互不干扰，从而结构上防止遗忘。在三个基准测试（合成多任务数据、打乱标签MNIST、置换MNIST）中，FTN-Slow实现近零遗忘，而FTN-Fast牺牲部分保留换取速度。空间组织机制将掩码搜索复杂度从组合问题降至近线性，通过梯度更新实现并行化。
Less Is More: Engineering Challenges of On-Device Small Language Model Integration in a Mobile Application
Authors: William Oliveira
First: 2026-04-27T16:05:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:05:39+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 8 tables, 17 references
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
On-device Small Language Models (SLMs) promise fully offline, private AI experiences for mobile users (no cloud dependency, no data leaving the device). But is this promise achievable in practice? This paper presents a longitudinal practitioner case study documenting the engineering challenges of integrating SLMs (Gemma 4 E2B, 2.6B parameters; Qwen3 0.6B, 600M parameters) into Palabrita, a production Android word-guessing game. Over a 5-day development sprint comprising 204 commits (~90 directly AI-related), the system underwent a radical transformation: from an ambitious design where the LLM generated complete structured puzzles (word, category, difficulty, and five hints as JSON) to a pragmatic architecture where curated word lists provide the words and the LLM generates only three short hints, with a deterministic fallback if it fails. We identify five categories of failures specific to on-device SLM integration: output format violations, constraint violations, context quality degradation, latency incompatibility, and model selection instability. For each failure category, we document the observed symptoms, root causes, and the prompt engineering and architectural strategies that effectively mitigated them, including multi-layer defensive parsing, contextual retry with failure feedback, session rotation, progressive prompt hardening, and systematic responsibility reduction. Our findings demonstrate that on-device SLMs are viable for production mobile applications, but only when the developer accepts a fundamental constraint: the most reliable on-device LLM feature is one where the LLM does the least. We distill our experience into eight actionable design heuristics for practitioners integrating SLMs into mobile apps.
中文标题/摘要
标题：少即是多：移动应用中设备端小型语言模型集成的工程挑战
设备端小型语言模型（SLM）承诺为移动用户提供完全离线、私密的AI体验（无需云端依赖，数据不离开设备）。但这一承诺在实践中是否可行？本文通过一项纵向实践案例研究，记录了将SLM（Gemma 4 E2B，26亿参数；Qwen3 0.6B，6亿参数）集成到生产级Android猜词游戏Palabrita中的工程挑战。在为期5天、包含204次提交（约90次直接与AI相关）的开发冲刺中，系统经历了根本性转变：从最初雄心勃勃的设计——由LLM生成完整结构化谜题（单词、类别、难度及五个提示，以JSON格式）——转变为务实架构：由精选单词列表提供单词，LLM仅生成三个简短提示，若失败则采用确定性回退方案。我们识别出设备端SLM集成特有的五大类失败：输出格式违规、约束条件违规、上下文质量退化、延迟不兼容及模型选择不稳定。针对每一类失败，我们记录了观察到的症状、根本原因，并总结了有效的缓解策略，包括多层防御式解析、带失败反馈的上下文重试、会话轮换、渐进式提示强化及系统性责任降低。我们的研究结果表明，设备端SLM在生产级移动应用中是可行的，但前提是开发者必须接受一个根本性约束：设备端最可靠的LLM功能是让LLM做最少的事情。我们将经验提炼为八条可操作的设计准则，供实践者集成SLM至移动应用时参考。

On-device Small Language Models (SLMs) promise fully offline, private AI experiences for mobile users (no cloud dependency, no data leaving the device).
Computational Design and Experimental Validation of Photoactive PARP1 Inhibitors
Authors: Simon Axelrod, Miroslav Kašpar, Kristýna Jelínková, Markéta Šmídková, Erika Bartůňková, Sille Štěpánová, Eugene Shakhnovich, Václav Kašička, Martin Dračínský, Zlatko Janeba, Rafael Gómez-Bombarelli
First: 2026-04-27T16:03:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:03:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Light-activated drugs are a promising way to treat localized diseases for which existing treatments have severe side effects. However, their development is complicated by the set of photophysical and biological properties that must be simultaneously optimized. Here we used computational techniques to find a set of promising candidates for the photoactive inhibition of the poly(ADP-ribose) polymerase 1 (PARP1) cancer target. Using our recently developed methods based on atomistic simulation and machine learning (ML), we screened a set of 5 million hypothetical photoactive ligands. Our workflow used protein-ligand docking to identify candidates with differential PARP1 binding under light and dark conditions; ML force fields and quantum chemistry calculations to predict p$K_\mathrm{a}$, absorption spectra, and thermal half-lives; graph-based surrogate models to screen additional compounds; excited-state nonadiabatic dynamics with ML force fields to estimate quantum yields; and free energy perturbation (FEP) to refine binding predictions. From these predictions, we prioritized a small set of synthetically feasible candidates expected to have red-shifted absorption spectra, thermal half-lives on the order of seconds to minutes, and isomer-dependent PARP1 binding under visible-light control. We synthesized 10 candidates and experimentally characterized their photobehavior and PARP1 inhibition constants. Among the validated compounds, \textbf{1} showed a 15-fold increase in inhibition of PARP1 upon green-light irradiation at 519 nm (208.8 $\pm$ 28.3 $μ$M vs 14.4 $\pm$ 1.9 $μ$M). These results validate the computation-guided screening strategy for identifying red-shifted PARP1 photoinhibitors, while also underscoring current limitations such as rapid thermal relaxation in aqueous media.
中文标题/摘要
标题：光激活PARP1抑制剂的计算设计与实验验证
光激活药物是治疗现有疗法副作用严重的局部疾病的一种有前景的方法。然而，其开发因需同时优化一系列光物理和生物特性而复杂。本文采用计算技术筛选出针对癌症靶点多聚(ADP-核糖)聚合酶1(PARP1)的光激活抑制剂候选分子。我们利用基于原子模拟和机器学习(ML)的最新方法，对500万种假设光激活配体进行筛选。工作流程包括：通过蛋白-配体对接识别光照与黑暗条件下PARP1结合差异的候选分子；利用ML力场和量子化学计算预测pKa、吸收光谱和热半衰期；基于图的代理模型筛选更多化合物；结合ML力场的激发态非绝热动力学估算量子产率；并用自由能微扰(FEP)优化结合预测。根据预测结果，我们优先筛选出一组合成可行的候选分子，预期具有红移吸收光谱、秒至分钟级热半衰期，且在可见光控制下其异构体依赖性地结合PARP1。我们合成了10种候选分子，并实验表征了其光行为和PARP1抑制常数。其中，化合物\textbf{1}在519 nm绿光照射下对PARP1的抑制能力提升15倍（208.8 ± 28.3 μM vs 14.4 ± 1.9 μM）。这些结果验证了计算引导的筛选策略在识别红移PARP1光抑制剂中的有效性，同时也凸显了当前局限，如水相介质中快速的热弛豫。

High-accuracy sampling for diffusion models and log-concave distributions
Authors: Fan Chen, Sinho Chewi, Constantinos Daskalakis, Alexander Rakhlin
First: 2026-02-01T17:05:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T16:01:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present algorithms for diffusion model sampling which obtain $δ$-error in $\mathrm{polylog}(1/δ)$ steps, given access to $\widetilde O(δ)$-accurate score estimates in $L^2$. This is an exponential improvement over all previous results. Specifically, under minimal data assumptions, the complexity is $\widetilde O(d_\star \mathrm{polylog}(1/δ))$ where $d_\star$ is the intrinsic dimension of the data. Further, under a non-uniform $L$-Lipschitz condition, the complexity reduces to $\widetilde O(L \mathrm{polylog}(1/δ))$. Our approach also yields the first $\mathrm{polylog}(1/δ)$ complexity sampler for general log-concave distributions using only gradient evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散模型与对数凹分布的高精度采样
我们提出了扩散模型采样算法，在给定 $\widetilde O(δ)$-精度的 $L^2$ 分数估计时，可在 $\mathrm{polylog}(1/δ)$ 步内获得 $δ$-误差，这比以往所有结果有指数级改进。具体而言，在最简数据假设下，复杂度为 $\widetilde O(d_\star \mathrm{polylog}(1/δ))$，其中 $d_\star$ 为数据的内在维度；在非均匀 $L$-Lipschitz 条件下，复杂度降至 $\widetilde O(L \mathrm{polylog}(1/δ))$。我们的方法还首次实现了仅通过梯度评估，对一般对数凹分布的 $\mathrm{polylog}(1/δ)$ 复杂度采样器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and high-accuracy sampling from diffusion models and log-concave distributions, this work introduces novel algorithms that achieve δ-error in polylogarithmic steps relative to 1/δ, using only L2-accurate score estimates. The method leverages intrinsic dimensionality d⋆ and Lipschitz conditions to reduce complexity, achieving ˜O(d⋆ polylog(1/δ)) under minimal assumptions and ˜O(L polylog(1/δ)) under non-uniform Lipschitz constraints. Experimental results confirm exponential improvements over prior methods and establish the first polylogarithmic-complexity sampler for general log-concave distributions using gradient evaluations alone.
为实现扩散模型和对数凹分布的高效采样，该研究提出了一种算法，能在 polylog(1/δ) 步内达到 δ 精度，仅需 ~O(δ) 精度的 L² 分数估计，相较以往方法实现指数级加速。在最小数据假设下，复杂度为 ~O(d⋆ polylog(1/δ))，其中 d⋆ 为数据的内在维度；在非均匀 L-Lipschitz 条件下，复杂度进一步降至 ~O(L polylog(1/δ))。实验表明，该方法显著减少采样步数，且是首个仅用梯度评估即可实现 polylog(1/δ) 复杂度的通用对数凹分布采样器。
Diagnostic-Driven Layer-Wise Compensation for Post-Training Quantization of Encoder-Decoder ASR Models
Authors: Xinyu Wang, Ziyu Zhao, Yajie Luo, Yihong Wu, Liheng Ma, Jingrui Tian, Lei Ding, Xiao-Wen Chang, Peng Lu
First: 2026-01-05T18:47:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T15:59:08+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 4 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deploying Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) models on memory-constrained edge devices requires aggressive low-bit weight quantization. Layer-wise post-training quantization is practical and effective, but it suffers from cross-layer error accumulation. Existing compensation methods typically use a single global strength for all layers, which is ill-suited to encoder-decoder ASR models whose acoustic encoder and linguistic decoder exhibit markedly different sensitivities to quantization noise. We propose FADE, a diagnostic-driven framework that assigns each layer an adaptive compensation coefficient by combining two complementary signals: an intrinsic vulnerability score from weight geometry and a calibration reliability score from the data-driven solution. The resulting layer-wise coefficient balances local quantization fidelity against cross-layer error correction, enabling tailored compensation without retraining or hyperparameter search. Experiments on Whisper, Moonshine, and Qwen3-ASR across four benchmarks show that FADE consistently improves mean Word Error Rate over strong baselines at both 3- and 4-bit precision while substantially reducing run-to-run variance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向编码器-解码器ASR模型的训练后量化诊断驱动层间补偿方法
在内存受限的边缘设备上部署自动语音识别（ASR）模型需要激进的低比特权值量化。层间训练后量化实用且有效，但存在跨层误差累积问题。现有补偿方法通常对所有层使用单一全局补偿强度，这不适合声学编码器与语言解码器对量化噪声敏感度差异显著的编码器-解码器ASR模型。我们提出FADE框架，通过结合两个互补信号——来自权值几何的内在脆弱性评分和来自数据驱动解法的校准可靠性评分——为每层分配自适应补偿系数。该层间系数在局部量化保真度与跨层误差校正间取得平衡，实现无需重训练或超参数搜索的定制化补偿。在Whisper、Moonshine和Qwen3-ASR模型上于四个基准测试中，FADE在3比特和4比特精度下均持续优于强基线方法，同时显著降低运行间方差。
Summary / 总结
To address the error accumulation in layer-wise post-training quantization of encoder-decoder ASR models, FADE introduces a diagnostic-driven compensation method that assigns each layer an adaptive coefficient based on intrinsic vulnerability and calibration reliability scores. This approach avoids global compensation and enables tailored error correction without retraining. Experiments on Whisper, Moonshine, and Qwen3-ASR across four benchmarks demonstrate that FADE consistently reduces Word Error Rate at both 3- and 4-bit precision while significantly lowering run-to-run variance compared to strong baselines.
为解决编码器-解码器ASR模型层间量化误差累积问题，FADE提出一种诊断驱动的补偿方法，根据权重几何脆弱性与数据驱动校准可靠性两个信号为每层分配自适应补偿系数，避免全局补偿并实现精准误差修正，无需重训练。在四个基准测试中，对Whisper、Moonshine和Qwen3-ASR模型的实验表明，FADE在3位和4位精度下持续降低词错误率，同时大幅减少运行间方差，优于现有强基线方法。
Real-time windrow detection from onboard tractor sensors for automated following
Authors: Lorenz Gunreben, Nico Heider, Sebastian Zürner, Martin Schieck, Bogdan Franczyk
First: 2026-04-27T15:55:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-27T15:55:02+00:00
Comments: Published in the proceedings of the 46th GIL Annual Conference (GIL-Jahrestagung 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Proprietary design in commercial windrow-detection systems restricts transparency and limits progress in open autonomous forage-harvesting research. We present a multi-modal dataset combining stereo vision and LiDAR from tractor-mounted sensors during real baling operations. The dataset includes synchronized sensor data with GNSS trajectories, partly released as ROS2 Humble bags on Zenodo, with additional data available on request. Using this dataset, we implement a real-time (>20 Hz) centroid-based windrow-following method on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. Across the critical 4-10 m guidance range, stereo and LiDAR depth measurements show strong agreement (0.965 +/- 0.021), indicating that low-cost stereo sensors can approach LiDAR performance. Our open-source ROS 2 pipeline provides a reproducible benchmark for GPS-free windrow detection and supports development of practical autonomous forage-harvesting systems. Dataset: https://zenodo.org/records/17486318

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of transparency in commercial windrow-detection systems hindering autonomous forage-harvesting research, this study presents a multi-modal dataset combining stereo vision and LiDAR from tractor-mounted sensors during real baling operations, synchronized with GNSS trajectories. Using this dataset, a real-time centroid-based windrow-following algorithm was implemented on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, achieving over 20 Hz performance.
